lam_bp_origin_clear_effect = {
	remove_variable = lam_bp_origin_topic
	remove_variable = lam_bp_origin_side
	remove_variable = lam_bp_origin_target
	remove_variable = lam_bp_origin_alternative
	remove_variable = lam_bp_origin_lord
	remove_variable = lam_bp_origin_object
	remove_variable = lam_bp_origin_detail
	remove_variable = lam_bp_origin_parent
	remove_variable = lam_bp_origin_founder
	remove_variable = lam_bp_origin_year
	remove_variable = lam_bp_origin_month
	remove_variable = lam_bp_origin_stem_nm
	remove_variable = lam_bp_origin_stem_ag
	remove_variable = lam_bp_origin_stem_rg
	remove_variable = lam_bp_origin_stem_hs
	remove_variable = lam_bp_origin_stem_fa
	remove_variable = lam_bp_origin_stem_cu
	remove_variable = lam_bp_origin_stem_person
	remove_variable = lam_bp_policy_until
}
lam_bp_foundation_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = lam_bp_position_topic
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = lam_bp_origin_topic
			value = var:lam_bp_position_topic
		}
	}
	else = {
		remove_variable = lam_bp_origin_topic
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = lam_bp_position_side
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = lam_bp_origin_side
			value = var:lam_bp_position_side
		}
	}
	else = {
		remove_variable = lam_bp_origin_side
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = lam_bp_position_target
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = lam_bp_origin_target
			value = var:lam_bp_position_target
		}
	}
	else = {
		remove_variable = lam_bp_origin_target
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = lam_bp_position_alternative
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = lam_bp_origin_alternative
			value = var:lam_bp_position_alternative
		}
	}
	else = {
		remove_variable = lam_bp_origin_alternative
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = lam_bp_position_lord
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = lam_bp_origin_lord
			value = var:lam_bp_position_lord
		}
	}
	else = {
		remove_variable = lam_bp_origin_lord
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = lam_bp_position_object
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = lam_bp_origin_object
			value = var:lam_bp_position_object
		}
	}
	else = {
		remove_variable = lam_bp_origin_object
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = lam_bp_position_detail
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = lam_bp_origin_detail
			value = var:lam_bp_position_detail
		}
	}
	else = {
		remove_variable = lam_bp_origin_detail
	}
	lam_cu_foundation_cause_effect = yes
	set_variable = {
		name = lam_bp_origin_parent
		value = scope:lam_vt_head
	}
	set_variable = {
		name = lam_bp_origin_founder
		value = this
	}
	set_variable = {
		name = lam_bp_origin_year
		value = current_year
	}
	set_variable = {
		name = lam_bp_origin_month
		value = lam_now_m
	}
	set_variable = {
		name = lam_bp_policy_until
		value = {
			value = lam_now_m
			add = 120
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:lam_vt_head = {
				has_variable = lam_bp_origin_stem_person
				OR = {
					NOT = {
						has_variable = lam_fac_nmv
					}
					var:lam_fac_nmv = 0
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:lam_vt_head = {
					has_variable = lam_bp_origin_stem_nm
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = lam_bp_origin_stem_nm
				value = scope:lam_vt_head.var:lam_bp_origin_stem_nm
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:lam_vt_head = {
					has_variable = lam_bp_origin_stem_ag
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = lam_bp_origin_stem_ag
				value = scope:lam_vt_head.var:lam_bp_origin_stem_ag
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:lam_vt_head = {
					has_variable = lam_bp_origin_stem_rg
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = lam_bp_origin_stem_rg
				value = scope:lam_vt_head.var:lam_bp_origin_stem_rg
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:lam_vt_head = {
					has_variable = lam_bp_origin_stem_hs
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = lam_bp_origin_stem_hs
				value = scope:lam_vt_head.var:lam_bp_origin_stem_hs
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:lam_vt_head = {
					has_variable = lam_bp_origin_stem_fa
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = lam_bp_origin_stem_fa
				value = scope:lam_vt_head.var:lam_bp_origin_stem_fa
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:lam_vt_head = {
					has_variable = lam_bp_origin_stem_cu
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = lam_bp_origin_stem_cu
				value = scope:lam_vt_head.var:lam_bp_origin_stem_cu
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:lam_vt_head = {
					has_variable = lam_bp_origin_stem_person
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = lam_bp_origin_stem_person
				value = scope:lam_vt_head.var:lam_bp_origin_stem_person
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = {
			name = lam_bp_origin_stem_person
			value = scope:lam_vt_head
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:lam_vt_head = {
					has_variable = lam_fac_nmv
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = lam_bp_origin_stem_nm
				value = scope:lam_vt_head.var:lam_fac_nmv
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:lam_vt_head = {
					has_variable = lam_fac_agenda
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = lam_bp_origin_stem_ag
				value = scope:lam_vt_head.var:lam_fac_agenda
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:lam_vt_head = {
					has_variable = lam_fac_region
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = lam_bp_origin_stem_rg
				value = scope:lam_vt_head.var:lam_fac_region
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:lam_vt_head.house
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = lam_bp_origin_stem_hs
				value = scope:lam_vt_head.house
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:lam_vt_head.faith
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = lam_bp_origin_stem_fa
				value = scope:lam_vt_head.faith
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:lam_vt_head.culture
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = lam_bp_origin_stem_cu
				value = scope:lam_vt_head.culture
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			var:lam_bp_origin_side ?= flag:restraint
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = lam_fac_wing
			value = flag:moderate
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			var:lam_bp_origin_side ?= flag:pressure
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = lam_fac_wing
			value = flag:hard
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			var:lam_bp_origin_side ?= flag:fair
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = lam_fac_wing
			value = flag:reform
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = {
			name = lam_fac_wing
			value = flag:new
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			var:lam_bp_origin_side ?= flag:retain
			var:lam_bp_origin_alternative ?= {
				is_alive = yes
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = lam_fac_heir
			value = var:lam_bp_origin_alternative
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			var:lam_bp_origin_topic ?= flag:heir
		}
		remove_variable = lam_fac_heir
	}
	lam_cu_foundation_heir_effect = yes
	lam_bp_archive_effect = yes
	lam_bp_record_found_effect = yes
	lam_gn_refresh_effect = yes
}
lam_bp_origin_pass_effect = {
	$TO$ = {
		lam_bp_origin_clear_effect = yes
	}
	$FROM$ = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_bp_origin_topic
			}
			$TO$ = {
				set_variable = {
					name = lam_bp_origin_topic
					value = $FROM$.var:lam_bp_origin_topic
				}
			}
		}
	}
	$FROM$ = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_bp_origin_side
			}
			$TO$ = {
				set_variable = {
					name = lam_bp_origin_side
					value = $FROM$.var:lam_bp_origin_side
				}
			}
		}
	}
	$FROM$ = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_bp_origin_target
			}
			$TO$ = {
				set_variable = {
					name = lam_bp_origin_target
					value = $FROM$.var:lam_bp_origin_target
				}
			}
		}
	}
	$FROM$ = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_bp_origin_alternative

lam_bp_archive_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = lam_bp_origin_topic
			has_variable = lam_fac_rec
			exists = var:lam_fac_rec_title
		}
		save_scope_as = lam_bp_record_head
		var:lam_fac_rec_title = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:lam_bp_record_head.var:lam_fac_rec = 1
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:lam_bp_record_head = {
							has_variable = lam_bp_origin_topic
						}
					}
					set_variable = {
						name = lam_gn1_bp_topic
						value = scope:lam_bp_record_head.var:lam_bp_origin_topic
					}
				}
				else = {
					remove_variable = lam_gn1_bp_topic
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:lam_bp_record_head = {
							has_variable = lam_bp_origin_side
						}
					}
					set_variable = {
						name = lam_gn1_bp_side
						value = scope:lam_bp_record_head.var:lam_bp_origin_side
					}
				}
				else = {
					remove_variable = lam_gn1_bp_side
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:lam_bp_record_head = {
							has_variable = lam_bp_origin_target
						}
					}
					set_variable = {
						name = lam_gn1_bp_target
						value = scope:lam_bp_record_head.var:lam_bp_origin_target
					}
				}
				else = {
					remove_variable = lam_gn1_bp_target
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:lam_bp_record_head = {
							has_variable = lam_bp_origin_alternative
						}
					}
					set_variable = {
						name = lam_gn1_bp_alternative
						value = scope:lam_bp_record_head.var:lam_bp_origin_alternative
					}
				}
				else = {
					remove_variable = lam_gn1_bp_alternative
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:lam_bp_record_head = {
							has_variable = lam_bp_origin_lord
						}
					}
					set_variable = {
						name = lam_gn1_bp_lord
						value = scope:lam_bp_record_head.var:lam_bp_origin_lord
					}
				}
				else = {
					remove_variable = lam_gn1_bp_lord
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:lam_bp_record_head = {
							has_variable = lam_bp_origin_object
						}
					}
					set_variable = {
						name = lam_gn1_bp_object
						value = scope:lam_bp_record_head.var:lam_bp_origin_object
					}
				}
				else = {
					remove_variable = lam_gn1_bp_object
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:lam_bp_record_head = {
							has_variable = lam_bp_origin_detail
						}
					}
					set_variable = {
						name = lam_gn1_bp_detail
						value = scope:lam_bp_record_head.var:lam_bp_origin_detail
					}
				}
				else = {
					remove_variable = lam_gn1_bp_detail
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:lam_bp_record_head = {
							has_variable = lam_bp_origin_parent
						}
					}
					set_variable = {
						name = lam_gn1_bp_parent
						value = scope:lam_bp_record_head.var:lam_bp_origin_parent
					}
				}
				else = {
					remove_variable = lam_gn1_bp_parent
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:lam_bp_record_head = {
							has_variable = lam_bp_origin_founder
						}
					}
					set_variable = {
						name = lam_gn1_bp_founder
						value = scope:lam_bp_record_head.var:lam_bp_origin_founder
					}
				}
				else = {
					remove_variable = lam_gn1_bp_founder
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:lam_bp_record_head = {
							has_variable = lam_bp_origin_year
						}
					}
					set_variable = {
						name = lam_gn1_bp_year
						value = scope:lam_bp_record_head.var:lam_bp_origin_year
					}
				}
				else = {
					remove_variable = lam_gn1_bp_year
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:lam_bp_record_head = {
							has_variable = lam_bp_origin_month
						}
					}
					set_variable = {
						name = lam_gn1_bp_month
						value = scope:lam_bp_record_head.var:lam_bp_origin_month
					}
				}
				else = {
					remove_variable = lam_gn1_bp_month
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:lam_bp_record_head = {
							has_variable = lam_bp_origin_stem_nm
						}
					}
					set_variable = {
						name = lam_gn1_bp_stem_nm
						value = scope:lam_bp_record_head.var:lam_bp_origin_stem_nm
					}
				}
				else = {
					remove_variable = lam_gn1_bp_stem_nm
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:lam_bp_record_head = {
							has_variable = lam_bp_origin_stem_ag
						}
					}
					set_variable = {
						name = lam_gn1_bp_stem_ag
						value = scope:lam_bp_record_head.var:lam_bp_origin_stem_ag
					}
				}
				else = {
					remove_variable = lam_gn1_bp_stem_ag
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:lam_bp_record_head = {
							has_variable = lam_bp_origin_stem_rg
						}
					}
					set_variable = {
						name = lam_gn1_bp_stem_rg
						value = scope:lam_bp_record_head.var:lam_bp_origin_stem_rg
					}
				}
				else = {
					remove_variable = lam_gn1_bp_stem_rg
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:lam_bp_record_head = {
							has_variable = lam_bp_origin_stem_hs
						}
					}
					set_variable = {
						name = lam_gn1_bp_stem_hs
						value = scope:lam_bp_record_head.var:lam_bp_origin_stem_hs
					}
				}
				else = {
					remove_variable = lam_gn1_bp_stem_hs
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:lam_bp_record_head = {
							has_variable = lam_bp_origin_stem_fa
						}
					}
					set_variable = {
						name = lam_gn1_bp_stem_fa
						value = scope:lam_bp_record_head.var:lam_bp_origin_stem_fa
					}
				}
				else = {
					remove_variable = lam_gn1_bp_stem_fa
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:lam_bp_record_head = {
							has_variable = lam_bp_origin_stem_cu
						}
					}
					set_variable = {
						name = lam_gn1_bp_stem_cu
						value = scope:lam_bp_record_head.var:lam_bp_origin_stem_cu
					}
				}
				else = {
					remove_variable = lam_gn1_bp_stem_cu
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:lam_bp_record_head = {
							has_variable = lam_bp_origin_stem_person
						}
					}
					set_variable = {
						name = lam_gn1_bp_stem_person
						value = scope:lam_bp_record_head.var:lam_bp_origin_stem_person
					}
				}
				else = {
					remove_variable = lam_gn1_bp_stem_person
				}
